```
     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any, Literal
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    21→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    22→    create_structured_llm,
    23→    parse_structured_output,
    24→)
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→# Mapping from legacy/LLM relation operators to the frontend's RelationOperator set
    29→_RELATION_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    30→    "has": "contains",
    31→    "is": "=",
    32→    "not": "not_contains",
    33→    "==": "=",
    34→    "range": "within",
    35→}
    36→
    37→
    38→def _normalize_relation(rel: str) -> str:
    39→    """Normalize a relation operator to the frontend's accepted set.
    40→
    41→    Maps legacy operators (has, is, not, ==, range) to the standard set:
    42→    =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains.
    43→    """
    44→    return _RELATION_MAP.get(rel, rel)
    45→
    46→
    47→class FieldMappingValue(BaseModel):
    48→    """Typed value object for field mappings with type discriminator.
    49→
    50→    Supports three value shapes:
    51→    - standard: single value + unit (e.g. HbA1c < 7%)
    52→    - range: min/max + unit (e.g. Age 18-65 years)
    53→    - temporal: duration + unit (e.g. within 6 months)
    54→    """
    55→
    56→    type: Literal["standard", "range", "temporal"] = Field(
    57→        description="Value type discriminator"
    58→    )
    59→    value: str | None = Field(
    60→        default=None, description="Value for standard type (e.g. '7')"
    61→    )
    62→    unit: str | None = Field(
    63→        default=None, description="Unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'months')"
    64→    )
    65→    min: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Minimum value for range type")
    66→    max: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Maximum value for range type")
    67→    duration: str | None = Field(
    68→        default=None, description="Duration value for temporal type"
    69→    )
    70→
    71→
    72→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    73→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    74→
    75→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
    76→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
    77→    """
    78→
    79→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
    80→    relation: str = Field(
    81→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'contains')"
    82→    )
    83→    value: FieldMappingValue = Field(
    84→        description="Typed value object with type discriminator"
    85→    )
    86→    unit: str | None = Field(
    87→        default=None,
    88→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
    89→    )
    90→
    91→
    92→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
    93→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
    94→
    95→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
    96→        default_factory=list,
    97→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
    98→    )
    99→
   100→
   101→async def generate_field_mappings(
   102→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
   103→    criterion_text: str,
   104→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   105→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
   106→
   107→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
   108→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
   109→
   110→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
   111→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
   112→
   113→    Args:
   114→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
   115→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   116→
   117→    Returns:
   118→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value,
   119→        entity_code, entity_system, omop_concept_id, entity_type.
   120→        Empty list if generation fails.
   121→    """
   122→    if not criterion_text:
   123→        return []
   124→
   125→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
   126→    if structured_llm is None:
   127→        return []
   128→
   129→    try:
   130→        # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
   131→        grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
   132→        code_context = ""
   133→        if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
   134→            system = entity.selected_system.upper()
   135→            code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
   136→
   137→        prompt = (
   138→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Decompose the"
   139→            " following criterion into structured AutoCriteria field"
   140→            " mappings using the Entity-Relation-Value-Unit pattern.\n\n"
   141→            f"Medical entity: {grounded_term} {code_context}\n"
   142→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   143→            "Instructions:\n"
   144→            "- Extract each discrete measurement, threshold, or condition"
   145→            " as a separate mapping\n"
   146→            "- entity: the specific measurement or concept"
   147→            " (e.g. 'HbA1c', 'Age', 'eGFR')\n"
   148→            "- relation: MUST be one of: =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within,"
   149→            " not_in_last, contains, not_contains\n"
   150→            '- value: a typed object with a "type" discriminator:\n'
   151→            '  - Standard: {"type": "standard", "value": "7", "unit": "%"}\n'
   152→            '  - Range: {"type": "range", "min": "18", "max": "65",'
   153→            ' "unit": "years"}\n'
   154→            '  - Temporal: {"type": "temporal", "duration": "6",'
   155→            ' "unit": "months"}\n'
   156→            "- Create one mapping per discrete condition in the criterion\n"
   157→            "- If no clear measurement exists, create one mapping with"
   158→            " relation='contains',"
   159→            ' value={"type": "standard", "value": "confirmed", "unit": ""}'
   160→        )
   161→
   162→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   163→        response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   164→
   165→        mappings = [
   166→            {
   167→                "entity": m.entity,
   168→                "relation": _normalize_relation(m.relation),
   169→                "value": m.value.model_dump(exclude_none=True),
   170→                "entity_code": entity.selected_code,
   171→                "entity_system": entity.selected_system,
   172→                "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   173→                "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   174→            }
   175→            for m in response.mappings
   176→        ]
   177→
   178→        logger.info(
   179→            "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   180→            len(mappings),
   181→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   182→        )
   183→        return mappings
   184→
   185→    except Exception as e:
   186→        logger.warning(
   187→            "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s': %s",
   188→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   189→            e,
   190→            exc_info=True,
   191→        )
   192→        # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   193→        return []
   194→
```

```
   167→def _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   168→    fm: dict[str, Any],
   169→    criterion_id: str,
   170→    protocol_id: str,
   171→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   172→    criterion_text: str,
   173→) -> AtomicCriterion:
   174→    """Create an AtomicCriterion record from a field_mapping dict.
   175→
   176→    Args:
   177→        fm: Field mapping dict with entity, relation, value, unit keys.
   178→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   179→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   180→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   181→        criterion_text: Original criterion text.
   182→
   183→    Returns:
   184→        AtomicCriterion instance (not yet added to session).
   185→    """
   186→    value_str = fm.get("value", "")
   187→    if not isinstance(value_str, str):
   188→        value_str = str(value_str) if value_str is not None else ""
   189→    value_numeric, value_text = _parse_value(value_str)
   190→
   191→    relation = fm.get("relation", "has")
   192→    negation = relation.upper() == "NOT" if relation else False
   193→
   194→    raw_unit = fm.get("unit")
   195→    _, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)
   196→
   197→    value_concept_id: int | None = None
   198→    entity_text = fm.get("entity")
   199→    raw_value = str(fm.get("value", ""))
   200→
   201→    # Ordinal-first: check if entity is an ordinal scale
   202→    ordinal_result = normalize_ordinal_value(raw_value, entity_text)
   203→    if ordinal_result is not None:
   204→        ordinal_value_cid, ordinal_unit_cid = ordinal_result
   205→        value_concept_id = ordinal_value_cid
   206→        if ordinal_unit_cid is not None:
   207→            unit_concept_id = ordinal_unit_cid
   208→    elif value_text and value_numeric is None:
   209→        _, value_concept_id = normalize_value(value_text)
   210→
   211→    # Derive entity_domain from entity_type when not explicitly provided
   212→    entity_domain = fm.get("entity_domain")
   213→    if not entity_domain:
   214→        entity_type = fm.get("entity_type", "")
```

```
     1→"""Pydantic schemas for expression tree logic detection and storage.
     2→
     3→Phase 2: Structured expression trees that capture AND/OR/NOT logic from
     4→criteria text. Two schema categories:
     5→
     6→1. LLM output schemas (LogicNode, LogicDetectionResponse) — Gemini structured
     7→   output for detecting logical structure in criterion text.
     8→2. JSONB storage schemas (ExpressionNode, StructuredCriterionTree) — stored
     9→   in Criteria.structured_criterion for UI rendering and CIRCE export.
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    15→
    16→
    17→class LogicNode(BaseModel):
    18→    """Tree node representing logical structure detected by Gemini.
    19→
    20→    ATOMIC nodes reference a field_mapping by index. AND/OR/NOT nodes
    21→    contain children. This schema is the Gemini structured output target.
    22→    """
    23→
    24→    node_type: str = Field(
    25→        description=(
    26→            "Logic operator: 'AND', 'OR', 'NOT', or 'ATOMIC'. "
    27→            "ATOMIC nodes are leaves referencing a field_mapping."
    28→        )
    29→    )
    30→    field_mapping_index: int | None = Field(
    31→        default=None,
    32→        description=(
    33→            "Index into the field_mappings list (0-based). Only set for ATOMIC nodes."
    34→        ),
    35→    )
    36→    children: list[LogicNode] | None = Field(
    37→        default=None,
    38→        description="Child nodes for AND/OR/NOT operators. None for ATOMIC.",
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→class LogicDetectionResponse(BaseModel):
    43→    """Gemini structured output for logic detection.
    44→
    45→    Contains the root of the logic tree and the model's reasoning
    46→    about why it chose this particular logical structure.
    47→    """
    48→
    49→    root: LogicNode = Field(description="Root node of the detected logic tree.")
    50→    reasoning: str = Field(
    51→        default="",
    52→        description="Explanation of the detected logical structure.",
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→class ExpressionNode(BaseModel):
    57→    """Node in the stored expression tree (JSONB in criteria.structured_criterion).
    58→
    59→    Leaf nodes carry atomic criterion details. Branch nodes carry the logic
    60→    operator and children. This is the serialization format for DB storage
    61→    and UI rendering.
    62→    """
    63→
    64→    type: str = Field(description="Node type: 'AND', 'OR', 'NOT', or 'ATOMIC'.")
    65→    atomic_criterion_id: str | None = Field(
    66→        default=None,
    67→        description="FK to atomic_criteria.id. Set for ATOMIC nodes only.",
    68→    )
    69→    entity: str | None = Field(
    70→        default=None,
    71→        description="Entity name from field_mapping. Set for ATOMIC nodes.",
    72→    )
    73→    relation: str | None = Field(
    74→        default=None,
    75→        description="Relation operator from field_mapping. Set for ATOMIC nodes.",
    76→    )
    77→    value: str | None = Field(
    78→        default=None,
    79→        description="Value from field_mapping. Set for ATOMIC nodes.",
    80→    )
    81→    unit: str | None = Field(
    82→        default=None,
    83→        description="Unit from field_mapping. Set for ATOMIC nodes.",
    84→    )
    85→    omop_concept_id: str | None = Field(
    86→        default=None,
    87→        description="OMOP concept ID for this atom. Set for ATOMIC nodes.",
    88→    )
    89→    children: list[ExpressionNode] | None = Field(
    90→        default=None,
    91→        description="Child expression nodes. Set for AND/OR/NOT nodes.",
    92→    )
    93→
    94→
    95→class StructuredCriterionTree(BaseModel):
    96→    """Top-level wrapper stored in Criteria.structured_criterion JSONB.
    97→
    98→    Contains the expression tree root, confidence source (llm vs fallback),
    99→    and the model name used for structure detection.
   100→    """

   102→    root: ExpressionNode = Field(description="Root node of the expression tree.")
```

```
     1→"""Structure builder tool for expression tree construction.
     2→
     3→Phase 2: Detects AND/OR/NOT logic in criterion text using Gemini,
     4→then builds normalized atomic_criteria/composite_criteria records
     5→and returns a StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage.
     6→
     7→Follows the field_mapper.py pattern: Gemini structured output with
     8→graceful fallback on failure.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from typing import Any
    16→
    17→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion, CompositeCriterion, CriterionRelationship
    18→from sqlmodel import Session
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.structure import (
    21→    ExpressionNode,
    22→    LogicDetectionResponse,
    23→    LogicNode,
    24→    StructuredCriterionTree,
    25→)
    26→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    27→    create_structured_llm,
    28→    parse_structured_output,
    29→)
    30→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import (
    31→    normalize_ordinal_value,
    32→    normalize_unit,
    33→    normalize_value,
    34→)
    35→
    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→# Map pipeline entity_type to OMOP-style entity_domain.
    39→# Used when field_mappings don't include an explicit entity_domain.
    40→_ENTITY_TYPE_TO_DOMAIN: dict[str, str] = {
    41→    "Condition": "condition",
    42→    "Medication": "drug",
    43→    "Lab_Value": "measurement",
    44→    "Procedure": "procedure",
    45→    "Demographic": "demographics",
    46→    "Other": "observation",
    47→}
    48→
    49→
    50→def _extract_value_fields(fm: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
    51→    """Extract flat value string and unit from a field mapping.
    52→
    53→    Handles both legacy flat strings and typed value dicts produced by
    54→    ``FieldMappingValue.model_dump(exclude_none=True)``.
    55→
    56→    Args:
    57→        fm: Field mapping dict with ``value`` (str or dict) and optional ``unit``.
    58→
    59→    Returns:
    60→        Tuple of (value_str, unit) where value_str is always a string
    61→        (possibly empty) and unit is the resolved unit string or None.
    62→    """
    63→    raw_value = fm.get("value", "")
    64→    top_unit = fm.get("unit")
    65→
    66→    if isinstance(raw_value, dict):
    67→        vtype = raw_value.get("type", "standard")
    68→        unit = raw_value.get("unit") or top_unit
    69→
    70→        if vtype == "range":
    71→            v_min = raw_value.get("min", "")
    72→            v_max = raw_value.get("max", "")
    73→            if v_min and v_max:
    74→                return f"{v_min}-{v_max}", unit
    75→            return v_min or v_max or "", unit
    76→        elif vtype == "temporal":
    77→            return raw_value.get("duration", ""), unit
    78→        else:  # standard
    79→            return raw_value.get("value", ""), unit
    80→
    81→    # Legacy: flat string
    82→    if not isinstance(raw_value, str):
    83→        raw_value = str(raw_value) if raw_value is not None else ""
    84→    return raw_value, top_unit
    85→
    86→
    87→def _parse_value(
    88→    raw_value: str,
    89→) -> tuple[float | None, str | None]:
    90→    """Parse a value string into numeric or text.
    91→
    92→    Tries float conversion first. If it succeeds, returns (numeric, None).
    93→    Otherwise returns (None, text).
    94→
    95→    Args:
    96→        raw_value: Raw value string from field_mapping.
    97→
    98→    Returns:
    99→        Tuple of (value_numeric, value_text) — one is always None.
   100→    """
   101→    try:
   102→        return (float(raw_value), None)
   103→    except (ValueError, TypeError):
   104→        return (None, raw_value)
   105→
   106→
   107→async def detect_logic_structure(
   108→    criterion_text: str,
   109→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   110→) -> LogicDetectionResponse | None:
   111→    """Detect AND/OR/NOT logic structure in criterion text using Gemini.
   112→
   113→    Skips LLM call if there's only 1 field_mapping (single atom, no logic).
   114→    Returns None on any failure (triggers fallback in caller).
   115→
   116→    Args:
   117→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
   118→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
   119→
   120→    Returns:
   121→        LogicDetectionResponse with the detected tree, or None on failure.
   122→    """
   123→    if len(field_mappings) <= 1:
   124→        return None
   125→
   126→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(LogicDetectionResponse)
   127→    if structured_llm is None:
   128→        return None
   129→
   130→    try:
   131→        # Build field mapping descriptions for context
   132→        mapping_lines = []
   133→        for i, fm in enumerate(field_mappings):
   134→            entity = fm.get("entity", "?")
   135→            relation = fm.get("relation", "?")
   136→            val_str, unit_str = _extract_value_fields(fm)
   137→            value = val_str or "?"
   138→            unit_suffix = f" {unit_str}" if unit_str else ""
   139→            mapping_lines.append(f"  [{i}] {entity} {relation} {value}{unit_suffix}")
   140→        mappings_text = "\n".join(mapping_lines)
   141→
   142→        prompt = (
   143→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Analyze the logical"
   144→            " structure of this eligibility criterion.\n\n"
   145→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   146→            f"Field mappings (indexed):\n{mappings_text}\n\n"
   147→            "Instructions:\n"
   148→            "- Determine how the field mappings are logically connected\n"
   149→            "- Use AND when all conditions must be met simultaneously\n"
   150→            "- Use OR when any one condition suffices\n"
   151→            "- Use NOT to negate a condition\n"
   152→            "- Use ATOMIC for leaf nodes that reference a single"
   153→            " field_mapping by index\n"
   154→            "- Return a tree where the root is AND, OR, NOT, or ATOMIC\n"
   155→            "- Every ATOMIC node must have field_mapping_index set to a"
   156→            " valid index (0 to "
   157→            f"{len(field_mappings) - 1})\n"
   158→            "- Each field_mapping index should appear exactly once\n"
   159→        )
   160→
   161→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   162→        response = parse_structured_output(result, LogicDetectionResponse)
   163→
   164→        # Validate all field_mapping_index values are in range
   165→        if not _validate_logic_tree(response.root, len(field_mappings)):
   166→            logger.warning(
   167→                "Logic tree validation failed for '%s' — invalid indices",
   168→                criterion_text[:50],
   169→            )
   170→            return None
   171→
   172→        return response
   173→
   174→    except Exception as e:
   175→        logger.warning(
   176→            "Logic detection failed for '%s': %s",
   177→            criterion_text[:50],
   178→            e,
   179→            exc_info=True,
   180→        )
   181→        return None
   182→
   183→
   184→def _validate_logic_tree(node: LogicNode, num_mappings: int) -> bool:
   185→    """Validate that all field_mapping_index values are in range.
   186→
   187→    Args:
   188→        node: Root LogicNode to validate.
   189→        num_mappings: Number of field_mappings (valid range: 0..num_mappings-1).
   190→
   191→    Returns:
   192→        True if all indices are valid, False otherwise.
   193→    """
   194→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   195→        if node.field_mapping_index is None:
   196→            return False
   197→        return 0 <= node.field_mapping_index < num_mappings
   198→    if node.children:
   199→        return all(_validate_logic_tree(child, num_mappings) for child in node.children)
   200→    # Non-ATOMIC with no children is invalid (AND/OR/NOT need children)
   201→    return False
   202→
   203→
   204→def _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   205→    fm: dict[str, Any],
   206→    criterion_id: str,
   207→    protocol_id: str,
   208→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   209→    criterion_text: str,
   210→) -> AtomicCriterion:
   211→    """Create an AtomicCriterion record from a field_mapping dict.
   212→
   213→    Args:
   214→        fm: Field mapping dict with entity, relation, value, unit keys.
   215→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   216→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   217→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   218→        criterion_text: Original criterion text.
   219→
   220→    Returns:
   221→        AtomicCriterion instance (not yet added to session).
   222→    """
   223→    value_str, raw_unit = _extract_value_fields(fm)
   224→    value_numeric, value_text = _parse_value(value_str)
   225→
   226→    relation = fm.get("relation", "has")
   227→    negation = relation.upper() == "NOT" if relation else False
   228→
   229→    _, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)
   230→
   231→    value_concept_id: int | None = None
   232→    entity_text = fm.get("entity")
   233→
   234→    # Ordinal-first: check if entity is an ordinal scale
   235→    ordinal_result = normalize_ordinal_value(value_str, entity_text)
   236→    if ordinal_result is not None:
   237→        ordinal_value_cid, ordinal_unit_cid = ordinal_result
   238→        value_concept_id = ordinal_value_cid
   239→        if ordinal_unit_cid is not None:
   240→            unit_concept_id = ordinal_unit_cid
   241→    elif value_text and value_numeric is None:
   242→        _, value_concept_id = normalize_value(value_text)
   243→
   244→    # Derive entity_domain from entity_type when not explicitly provided
   245→    entity_domain = fm.get("entity_domain")
   246→    if not entity_domain:
   247→        entity_type = fm.get("entity_type", "")
   248→        entity_domain = _ENTITY_TYPE_TO_DOMAIN.get(entity_type)
   249→
   250→    return AtomicCriterion(
   251→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   252→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   253→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   254→        entity_concept_id=fm.get("entity_concept_id"),
   255→        entity_concept_system=fm.get("entity_concept_system"),
   256→        omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   257→        entity_domain=entity_domain,
   258→        relation_operator=relation,
   259→        value_numeric=value_numeric,
   260→        value_text=value_text,
   261→        unit_text=raw_unit,
   262→        unit_concept_id=unit_concept_id,
   263→        value_concept_id=value_concept_id,
   264→        negation=negation,
   265→        original_text=criterion_text,
   266→        confidence_score=fm.get("confidence_score"),
   267→    )
   268→
   269→
   270→def _build_tree_from_logic(
   271→    node: LogicNode,
   272→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   273→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion],
   274→    criterion_id: str,
   275→    protocol_id: str,
   276→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   277→    session: Session,
   278→) -> tuple[ExpressionNode, str]:
   279→    """Recursively build ExpressionNode tree and DB records from LogicNode.
   280→
   281→    Creates CompositeCriterion and CriterionRelationship records for
   282→    branch nodes. Leaf nodes reference already-created AtomicCriterion
   283→    records by index.
   284→
   285→    Args:
   286→        node: Current LogicNode from LLM output.
   287→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts.
   288→        atomic_records: Pre-created AtomicCriterion records (indexed).
   289→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   290→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   291→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   292→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   293→
   294→    Returns:
   295→        Tuple of (ExpressionNode for JSONB storage, node's DB record ID).
   296→    """
   297→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   298→        idx = node.field_mapping_index or 0
   299→        atomic = atomic_records[idx]
   300→        fm = field_mappings[idx]
   301→        val_str, unit_str = _extract_value_fields(fm)
   302→        expr = ExpressionNode(
   303→            type="ATOMIC",
   304→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   305→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   306→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   307→            value=val_str or None,
   308→            unit=unit_str,
   309→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   310→        )
   311→        return expr, atomic.id
   312→
   313→    # Branch node: create CompositeCriterion
   314→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   315→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   316→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   317→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   318→        logic_operator=node.node_type,
   319→    )
   320→    session.add(composite)
   321→    session.flush()
   322→
   323→    children_nodes: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   324→    for seq, child in enumerate(node.children or []):
   325→        child_expr, child_db_id = _build_tree_from_logic(
   326→            child,
   327→            field_mappings,
   328→            atomic_records,
   329→            criterion_id,
   330→            protocol_id,
   331→            inclusion_exclusion,
   332→            session,
   333→        )
   334→        children_nodes.append(child_expr)
   335→
   336→        child_type = "atomic" if child.node_type == "ATOMIC" else "composite"
   337→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   338→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   339→            child_criterion_id=child_db_id,
   340→            child_type=child_type,
   341→            child_sequence=seq,
   342→        )
   343→        session.add(rel)
   344→
   345→    expr = ExpressionNode(
   346→        type=node.node_type,
   347→        children=children_nodes,
   348→    )
   349→    return expr, composite.id
   350→
   351→
   352→async def build_expression_tree(
   353→    criterion_text: str,
   354→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   355→    criterion_id: str,
   356→    protocol_id: str,
   357→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   358→    session: Session,
   359→) -> StructuredCriterionTree:
   360→    """Build an expression tree from criterion field_mappings.
   361→
   362→    Two-pass approach:
   363→    1. LLM pass: call detect_logic_structure() for AND/OR/NOT detection
   364→    2. Fallback: if LLM fails or single mapping, AND-of-all-atomics
   365→
   366→    Creates AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping, plus
   367→    CompositeCriterion + CriterionRelationship for tree structure.
   368→
   369→    Args:
   370→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
   371→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
   372→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   373→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   374→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   375→        session: Active SQLModel session (records are added but not committed).
   376→
   377→    Returns:
   378→        StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage in criteria.structured_criterion.
   379→    """
   380→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   381→
   382→    # Create AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping
   383→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion] = []
   384→    for fm in field_mappings:
   385→        atomic = _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   386→            fm, criterion_id, protocol_id, inclusion_exclusion, criterion_text
   387→        )
   388→        session.add(atomic)
   389→        session.flush()
   390→        atomic_records.append(atomic)
   391→
   392→    # Pass 1: LLM logic detection
   393→    logic_response = await detect_logic_structure(criterion_text, field_mappings)
   394→
   395→    if logic_response is not None:
   396→        # Build tree from LLM-detected structure
   397→        root_expr, _ = _build_tree_from_logic(
   398→            logic_response.root,
   399→            field_mappings,
   400→            atomic_records,
   401→            criterion_id,
   402→            protocol_id,
   403→            inclusion_exclusion,
   404→            session,
   405→        )
   406→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   407→            root=root_expr,
   408→            structure_confidence="llm",
   409→            structure_model=gemini_model_name,
   410→        )
   411→
   412→    # Pass 2: Fallback — AND-of-all-atomics (or single ATOMIC)
   413→    if len(atomic_records) == 1:
   414→        fm = field_mappings[0]
   415→        val_str, unit_str = _extract_value_fields(fm)
   416→        root_expr = ExpressionNode(
   417→            type="ATOMIC",
   418→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic_records[0].id,
   419→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   420→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   421→            value=val_str or None,
   422→            unit=unit_str,
   423→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   424→        )
   425→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   426→            root=root_expr,
   427→            structure_confidence="fallback",
   428→            structure_model=None,
   429→        )
   430→
   431→    # Multiple mappings, LLM failed → wrap in AND
   432→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   433→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   434→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   435→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   436→        logic_operator="AND",
   437→        original_text=criterion_text,
   438→    )
   439→    session.add(composite)
   440→    session.flush()
   441→
   442→    children: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   443→    for seq, (atomic, fm) in enumerate(zip(atomic_records, field_mappings)):
   444→        val_str, unit_str = _extract_value_fields(fm)
   445→        children.append(
   446→            ExpressionNode(
   447→                type="ATOMIC",
   448→                atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   449→                entity=fm.get("entity"),
   450→                relation=fm.get("relation"),
   451→                value=val_str or None,
   452→                unit=unit_str,
   453→                omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   454→            )
   455→        )
   456→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   457→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   458→            child_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   459→            child_type="atomic",
   460→            child_sequence=seq,
   461→        )
   462→        session.add(rel)
   463→
   464→    root_expr = ExpressionNode(type="AND", children=children)
   465→    return StructuredCriterionTree(
   466→        root=root_expr,
   467→        structure_confidence="fallback",
   468→        structure_model=None,
   469→    )
   470→
```

```
    65→def normalize_unit(unit_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    66→    """Normalize a unit string to its UCUM code and OMOP unit_concept_id.
    67→
    68→    Args:
    69→        unit_text: Raw unit text (e.g. "mg/dL", "%", "years").
    70→
    71→    Returns:
    72→        Tuple of (ucum_code, omop_unit_concept_id), or (None, None)
    73→        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.
    74→    """
    75→    if unit_text is not None and not isinstance(unit_text, str):
    76→        unit_text = str(unit_text)
    77→    if not unit_text or not unit_text.strip():
    78→        return (None, None)
    79→
    80→    key = unit_text.strip().lower()
    81→    unit_lookup, _ = _load_ucum_mappings()
    82→    result = unit_lookup.get(key)
    83→    if result is not None:
    84→        return result
    85→    return (None, None)
    86→
    87→
    88→def normalize_value(
    89→    value_text: str | None,
    90→) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    91→    """Normalize a categorical value to its SNOMED code and OMOP concept ID.
    92→
    93→    Args:
    94→        value_text: Raw value text (e.g. "positive", "negative", "normal").
    95→
    96→    Returns:
    97→        Tuple of (normalized_text, omop_value_concept_id), or (None, None)
    98→        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.
    99→    """
   100→    if value_text is not None and not isinstance(value_text, str):
   101→        value_text = str(value_text)
   102→    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():
   103→        return (None, None)
   104→
   105→    key = value_text.strip().lower()
   106→    _, value_lookup = _load_ucum_mappings()
   107→    concept_id = value_lookup.get(key)
   108→    if concept_id is not None:
   109→        return (key, concept_id)
   110→    return (None, None)
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Ordinal scale normalization (Phase 3b)
```

```
     1→"""Shared Gemini LLM utilities for protocol processor tools.
     2→
     3→Centralizes the repeated pattern of creating a ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
     4→client with structured output and parsing its response. Used by
     5→structure_builder, ordinal_resolver, and field_mapper.
     6→
     7→Falls back to Ollama when GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set but OLLAMA_BASE_URL
     8→is configured.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from typing import Any, TypeVar
    16→
    17→from pydantic import BaseModel
    18→
    19→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20→
    21→T = TypeVar("T", bound=BaseModel)
    22→
    23→
    24→def create_structured_llm(
    25→    output_schema: type[T],
    26→) -> Any | None:
    27→    """Create an LLM client with structured output.
    28→
    29→    Tries Gemini first (GOOGLE_API_KEY), falls back to Ollama
    30→    (OLLAMA_BASE_URL) if Gemini is not configured.
    31→
    32→    Args:
    33→        output_schema: Pydantic model class for structured output.
    34→
    35→    Returns:
    36→        A structured LLM instance, or None if no backend is available.
    37→    """
    38→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
    39→    if google_api_key:
    40→        try:
    41→            from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    42→
    43→            gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
    44→            gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    45→                model=gemini_model_name,
    46→                google_api_key=google_api_key,
    47→            )
    48→            return gemini.with_structured_output(output_schema)
    49→        except Exception as e:
    50→            logger.warning("Failed to create Gemini client: %s", e)
    51→
    52→    # Fallback to Ollama
    53→    ollama_base_url = os.getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL")
    54→    if ollama_base_url:
    55→        try:
    56→            from langchain_community.chat_models import ChatOllama
    57→
    58→            ollama_model = os.getenv("OLLAMA_MODEL", "gemma2:9b")
    59→            logger.info(
    60→                "Using Ollama for structured output: %s/%s",
    61→                ollama_base_url,
    62→                ollama_model,
    63→            )
    64→            ollama = ChatOllama(
    65→                base_url=ollama_base_url,
    66→                model=ollama_model,
    67→                format="json",
    68→                temperature=0.1,
    69→            )
    70→            return ollama.with_structured_output(output_schema)
    71→        except Exception as e:
    72→            logger.warning("Failed to create Ollama client: %s", e)
    73→
    74→    logger.warning("No LLM backend available — skipping structured output call")
    75→    return None
    76→
    77→
    78→def parse_structured_output(result: Any, model: type[T]) -> T:
    79→    """Parse a structured LLM output into a Pydantic model.
    80→
    81→    Handles both dict and already-parsed model instances returned
    82→    by LangChain's with_structured_output().
    83→
    84→    Args:
    85→        result: Raw LLM output (dict or model instance).
    86→        model: Pydantic model class to validate against.
    87→
    88→    Returns:
    89→        Validated model instance.
    90→    """
    91→    if isinstance(result, dict):
    92→        return model.model_validate(result)
    93→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
    94→
```

> AGENT

Yes — structured output is properly enforced. `gemini_utils.py` uses `.with_structured_output(output_schema)` at lines 48 and 70, which tells LangChain to use the Pydantic model as the response schema. The LLM is constrained to return JSON matching the schema, and `parse_structured_output` then validates it via `model.model_validate()`.

So the chain is:
1. `field_mapper.py` calls `create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)` — Gemini is constrained to return `FieldMappingResponse`
2. The response is parsed via `parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)` — Pydantic validates the shape
3. `FieldMappingItem.value` is typed as `FieldMappingValue` (Pydantic model) — so `m.value` is always a validated `FieldMappingValue`
4. Line 169: `m.value.model_dump(exclude_none=True)` serializes it to a dict

The structured output enforcement is correct and working. The issue was purely downstream — `structure_builder.py` expected `fm["value"]` to be a flat string but commit `05b6c5e` changed it to a typed dict. The `_extract_value_fields` helper I just added properly unwraps these dicts based on the `type` discriminator (`standard`, `range`, `temporal`), extracting the actual value string and unit.

> AGENT

Good — `gemini_extractor.py` doesn't consume field_mapping values directly. The fix is complete and scoped to `structure_builder.py`.

**Summary of what was done:**

The `'dict' object has no attribute 'strip'` error was caused by commit `05b6c5e` which changed `field_mapper.py` to produce typed `FieldMappingValue` dicts (e.g. `{"type": "standard", "value": "7", "unit": "%"}`) instead of flat strings. `structure_builder.py` was still treating `fm["value"]` as a string.

**Proper fix** (replacing the band-aid `str()` coercion):

- Added `_extract_value_fields(fm)` helper at `structure_builder.py:50` that handles all three value types:
  - **standard**: extracts `value` field (e.g. `"7"`)
  - **range**: combines `min`-`max` (e.g. `"18-65"`)
  - **temporal**: extracts `duration` (e.g. `"6"`)
  - Also resolves unit from the value dict, falling back to top-level `fm["unit"]`
  - Backward-compatible with legacy flat string values

- Updated all 6 consumption points in `structure_builder.py`:
  1. `_create_atomic_from_mapping` — value extraction + unit resolution
  2. `detect_logic_structure` — LLM prompt formatting
  3. `_build_tree_from_logic` — ExpressionNode construction
  4. `build_expression_tree` single-atom fallback
  5. `build_expression_tree` AND-fallback loop

- Pydantic `with_structured_output` is already properly enforced in `gemini_utils.py` — both Gemini and Ollama paths use it.

```json
  "old_string": "def _ensure_mlflow() -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Ensure MLflow tracking is configured in the current thread.\n\n    Returns True if MLflow tracing is available and configured.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        import mlflow\n\n        tracking_uri = os.getenv(\"MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI\")\n        if tracking_uri:\n            mlflow.set_tracking_uri(tracking_uri)\n            mlflow.set_experiment(\"protocol-processing\")\n            return True\n    except ImportError:\n        logger.warning(\"mlflow not installed — tracing disabled\")\n    return False",
  "new_string": "def _ensure_mlflow() -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Ensure MLflow tracking is configured in the current thread.\n\n    Handles the case where the experiment was soft-deleted by restoring it\n    before setting it as active. Returns True if MLflow tracing is available.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        import mlflow\n\n        tracking_uri = os.getenv(\"MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI\")\n        if tracking_uri:\n            mlflow.set_tracking_uri(tracking_uri)\n            try:\n                mlflow.set_experiment(\"protocol-processing\")\n            except mlflow.exceptions.MlflowException:\n                # Experiment was soft-deleted — restore it and retry.\n                client = mlflow.MlflowClient()\n                exp = client.get_experiment_by_name(\"protocol-processing\")\n                if exp and exp.lifecycle_stage == \"deleted\":\n                    logger.info(\"Restoring deleted MLflow experiment '%s'\", exp.experiment_id)\n                    client.restore_experiment(exp.experiment_id)\n                    mlflow.set_experiment(\"protocol-processing\")\n                else:\n                    raise\n            return True\n    except ImportError:\n        logger.warning(\"mlflow not installed — tracing disabled\")\n    except Exception:\n        logger.warning(\"MLflow setup failed — tracing disabled\", exc_info=True)\n    return False"
}
```

> AGENT

The fix:

1. **Auto-restore**: When `set_experiment` raises `MlflowException` (deleted experiment), it looks up the experiment by name, checks if it's in `deleted` lifecycle stage, and restores it via `client.restore_experiment()`. Then retries `set_experiment`.

2. **Graceful fallback**: If anything else goes wrong (not a deleted experiment, MLflow server down, etc.), the generic `except Exception` logs a warning and returns `False` — pipeline runs without tracing rather than crashing.

Restart your MLflow server (`make run-mlflow`) and the protocol processor. The next run will restore the experiment automatically.

```
     1→"""Unified outbox event handler for ProtocolUploaded events.
     2→
     3→Replaces the two-service trigger pattern (extraction_service + grounding_service)
     4→with a single consolidated handler that invokes the full 5-node pipeline:
     5→ingest -> extract -> parse -> ground -> persist
     6→
     7→Per user decision (v2.0): "Remove criteria_extracted outbox, retain protocol_uploaded"
     8→
     9→The handler is synchronous (called by OutboxProcessor.poll_and_process) and
    10→bridges to the async graph via asyncio.run(). This works without event loop
    11→conflicts because the outbox processor runs handlers in a thread executor
    12→via run_in_executor, so there is no existing event loop in the current thread.
    13→
    14→Checkpointing: Each invocation generates a unique thread_id (protocol_id:uuid4)
    15→and stores it in protocol.metadata_ so that retry_from_checkpoint can look it up.
    16→This prevents checkpoint collision when re-extracting the same protocol.
    17→"""
    18→
```
